# PC-98 collection index

4140 titles from 385 publisher archives in `E:\PC98`. **180 have a known English patch** (marked EN); 17 more have a translation in progress.

Generated by `tools/build_index.py`; sources are listed in `index/SOURCES.md`; engine families (`backticks`) come from `tools/engine_survey.py`, see `index/ENGINES.md`.

**♥ = adult content.** Every script is translated by a separate translation model, which also classifies it; this repo extracts the scripts and builds the patchers. The mark is provisional (VNDB or publisher record) until the script has been classified; each title's basis is in `index.json` (`heart_basis`).
